# Geometry validator audit

Validation ran with `run_spec.py` and no rendering for every JSON spec under each listed worktree. Each failed count is per spec file; repeated frame-level messages are collapsed into validator categories. Pilot and edition directories contain duplicate `elementKey` values in several trees; those files were run separately and are named with their directory below. No `anim-cx-*` specs were edited.

| Worktree / batch | Specs | Failed specs |
|---|---:|---:|
| `anim-cx-mag-10` | 620 | 323 |
| `anim-cx-mag-11` | 621 | 324 |
| `anim-cx-mag-12` | 628 | 331 |
| `anim-cx-mag-14` | 635 | 338 |
| `anim-cx-mag-3` | 610 | 342 |
| `anim-cx-mag-4` | 615 | 313 |
| `anim-cx-mag-5` | 600 | 303 |
| `anim-cx-mag-6` | 620 | 321 |
| `anim-cx-mag-7` | 617 | 320 |
| `anim-cx-mag-8` | 611 | 314 |
| `anim-cx-mag-9` | 598 | 268 |
| `anim-cx-wag-5` | 632 | 335 |
| `anim-cx-wag-8` | 616 | 314 |
| `anim-cx-wag-9` | 599 | 285 |
| `animaciones` | 563 | 266 |

**Total:** 9185 spec files across 15 worktrees; 4697 failed files.

The numbers count specs with at least one validator problem, not the number of frames or messages. The key lists below give the reason categories per batch. A key can appear under more than one category.
